- Repository at the pinned commit (may be consulted freely): https://github.com/jdubois/boot-ui/tree/85ec9a19f8f700c571d4ed3bd3b2919ff3de3ebd
- Location: [`bootui-engine/src/main/java/io/github/jdubois/bootui/engine/architecture/ArchitectureRules.java:2171`](https://github.com/jdubois/boot-ui/blob/85ec9a19f8f700c571d4ed3bd3b2919ff3de3ebd/bootui-engine/src/main/java/io/github/jdubois/bootui/engine/architecture/ArchitectureRules.java#L2171) · path class: main
- Rule: `transactions` (java-vibe-guard), severity critical
- Message: @Transactional + @Async — transaction will NOT propagate to async thread

## Enclosing function `rule` (lines 2168–2201)

```java
 2168      @Override
 2169      ArchRule rule(ArchitectureContext context) {
 2170          return classes()
>2171                  .should(new ArchCondition<JavaClass>("not combine @Async and @Transactional on one method") {
 2172                      @Override
 2173                      public void check(JavaClass javaClass, ConditionEvents events) {
 2174                          for (JavaMethod method : ArchitectureRuleSupport.declaredMethods(javaClass)) {
 2175                              if (!SpringStereotypes.ASYNC_ANNOTATED.test(method)
 2176                                      || !SpringStereotypes.TRANSACTIONAL_ANNOTATED.test(method)) {
 2177                                  continue;
 2178                              }
 2179                              context.evidence().observed();
 2180                              Optional<String> phase = transactionalEventListenerPhase(method);
 2181                              if (phase.isEmpty()) {
 2182                                  events.add(SimpleConditionEvent.violated(
 2183                                          method,
 2184                                          "Method " + method.getFullName()
 2185                                                  + " combines @Async and @Transactional; the transaction runs"
 2186                                                  + " on the async thread and the caller's context does not"
 2187                                                  + " propagate"));
 2188                              } else if (BEFORE_COMMIT_PHASE.equals(phase.get())) {
 2189                                  events.add(SimpleConditionEvent.violated(
 2190                                          method,
 2191                                          "Asynchronous transactional event listener " + method.getFullName()
 2192                                                  + " listens in the BEFORE_COMMIT phase; it runs on the async"
 2193                                                  + " thread while the publishing transaction is still open, so"
 2194                                                  + " its own transaction observes state that transaction has"
 2195                                                  + " not committed"));
 2196                              }
 2197                          }
 2198                      }
 2199                  })
 2200                  .as("Async and transactional semantics on one method should be reviewed");
 2201      }
```
